## Goal
- Goal ID: T109_GOAL_20260926_150_LORA_CANDIDATE_PRODUCTION

- Semantics: 150 designed tasks to be processed once; this is NOT a requirement for 150 successful or unique final images.
- Production Workers: interchangeable team pool
- QA: PAUSED
- MASTER DIRECTOR: design/queue only; never directly generate images
- Official reference: MASTER_IMAGE/INARIA_20_MASTER_v1.0.png

## Worker rules
1. Claim only a task whose status is QUEUED.
2. Read the latest Goal and Queue SHA before claiming.
3. Execute the assigned task exactly; do not redesign it.
4. Workers are GENERATE-ONLY. Do not self-QA, PASS/REPAIR/REJECT, or regenerate merely because the candidate is imperfect.
5. A successfully returned candidate is recorded as IMAGE_CREATED and the Worker is released.
6. GENERATION_TOOL_ERROR follows retry/circuit-breaker rules.
7. SAFETY_BLOCKED is recorded and skipped; never bypass it by rewriting the prompt.
8. A failed, blocked, or duplicate task may be replaced later by MASTER DIRECTOR with a new legitimate design.
9. Do not create new tasks yourself.
10. QA is paused and must not block production.
11. Front/side-facing only; no back-facing compositions.
12. No fully readable real-character + fully readable mirror/reflection composition.
13. Clothing diversity is mandatory; the original MASTER_IMAGE outfit is not the default outfit for this batch.
